Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The time variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 191 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 658 and intersection form of rank 1.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_191(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 191. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^658(S, F) is trivial. QED.
